# CONVERSION REGISTER: TODAY'S POTENTIAL EDGE AS TRADEABLE SLEEVES AND STRATEGIES

Owner order Sun 2026-11-15 19:37 ET: figure out rigorously how to turn the potential edge found today into actual sleeves and strategies that can be traded. Built Sun 2026-11-15 night by the Conversion Program under personal supervision: every register was recounted against its source before anything below was written, and no count in this file claims coverage beyond the fraction printed in its own sentence.

SCOPE. Surface: this register, one full specification per surviving candidate in the Edge repo's idiom (payer, execution net, decisive test, kill rule), and the wiring that was lawful tonight. Constraints: the desk trades shares on paper and Midna's book is options only under Charter v1.5 Amendment 16; the Tensor engine is untouched (Tensor-derived objects convert at the sleeve layer only); no sleeve is killed or parked by this program; nothing trades tonight on a historical grade alone; sizing is the desk's existing sleeve sizing on the desk and Amendment 16 stress-loss sizing on the book. Outcome: every tradeable object from Sat 2026-11-14 and Sun 2026-11-15 has a named conversion route with its expression, spec, sizing rule, falsifier, remaining proof and first-trade condition, and every non-convertible object names why and what would change that. Proof: this file and its JSONL twin, recounted, plus the wiring evidence in section 5.

### CONV-01 to CONV-06: the overnight family

#### CONV-01: C01 Overnight Focus-names edge
Source: register[0]. Tier: EXAM-PASSED. Surface: desk sleeve (shares, paper). Status: LIVE.
Route: Shares at the auctions only: MOC in from 15:50 ET, MOO out at the open, equal weight over the Focus 29 basket (10-name floor), any name whose auction access fails that night is dropped and never crossed. The options route was tested fresh tonight on current TastyTrade chains and is closed (see Execution net).
Expression: Long the basket overnight, closing auction print to opening auction print. No intraday leg (its Sharpe was 0.01). No options form.
Payer: Whoever must transact at the open regardless of price (index rebalancers, overnight news reactors, market-on-open flow) pays the overnight premium to whoever warehouses the names from the close cross to the open cross.
Execution net: Gross +16.46bp per night (2,229 sessions, t 4.97). Crossing spreads costs about 27bp a night and kills it; the auction form's measured cost is about 5.8bp on the carry names, leaving the registered 5bp friction standard as the honest planning figure. Options form, fresh test Sun 2026-11-15 on TastyTrade chains at the Fri 2026-11-13 close (pass2/CONVERSION-OPTIONS-TEST-2026-11-15.md): 38 costings across long ATM calls, one-step and 0.25-delta call spreads, and same-strike calendars on all five carry names, weekend and weeknight; 38 FAIL, 0 SURVIVES. Best expression (AMD near-zero-delta spread) misses by 12.34bp weekend; best expression with meaningful capture (SNDK 0.25-delta spread, capture 6.39bp) misses by 40.91bp. Wave 4's independent measurement on Alpaca chains agreed (NVDA ATM net -139.5bp, RKLB ATM -140.6bp). The options route is closed on two independent chain sources.
Sizing: NEWLY REGISTERED by this program (the waves registered implementation and grading but never capital sizing): the sleeve carries one standard desk sleeve slice under the desk's existing uniform slice sizing, split equally across the names present that night; no leverage; per-name weight is the slice divided by the surviving name count.
Decisive test: The running 60-session falsifier is the decisive test; nothing cheaper decides it, because the claim is a forward claim by construction.
Falsifier: 60 forward sessions from Mon 2026-11-16; DEMOTED if the mean net of 5bp is not positive at session 60 (zero demotes). The bar-based focus29 accrual owns the registered verdict; the carry5 auction-print tracker is the companion verdict and any divergence is itself a reported result.
Kill rule: Mean net of 5bp not positive at counted session 60 demotes the sleeve; the conditioner arms (CONV-48 to CONV-54) never rescue a demoted basket, they only grade its anatomy.
Remaining proof: The 60-session forward accrual itself (running; session 1 is Mon 2026-11-16). Placebo percentile attaches to the first family grade from the Placebo Fleet's registered seeds, per Lane C.
First-trade condition: At the session-60 read, if the verdict is SURVIVES on the bar accrual and the carry5 print companion is not DEMOTED, the sleeve rosters live on the desk under the sizing above. If the two verdicts diverge, the divergence is reported and the print verdict governs position size (print is the price a trader could actually have received).
Going-live disposition, Sun 2026-11-15 night (GOING-LIVE-20261115): class LIVE-SLEEVE. Rostered live as overnight_focus29 on desk3/overnight_baskets.py (Focus 29, 10-name floor, standard slice, dispatcher fills at close and open bars; auction prints remain the grading basis in the falsifier tracker). Session-60 falsifier accrual continues unchanged.

Source: register[12]. Tier: LIVE CANDIDATE. Surface: desk sleeve (shares, paper). Status: LIVE.

Expression: Membership in CONV-01, CONV-02 and CONV-05 baskets; per-name auction-print capture runs beside the falsifier from Mon 2026-11-16.
Payer: Same overnight warehousing payer as CONV-01.
Execution net: Per-name standing: ON_1n_FCEL DSR 0.9995, Sharpe 1.42, t 4.22, +0.420 percent per night gross; ON_1n_RKLB DSR 0.9997, Sharpe 1.59, t 4.74, +0.215 percent (with the AVOIDANCE-side note at register[158]: RKLB is the family's PBO carrier, an overfit signature to respect, not a promotion argument); ON_1n_TSM DSR 0.9970; remaining clearers DSR 0.9502 to 0.9874, Sharpe 1.05 to 1.27.
Sizing: Basket weights only; no standalone sizing registered.
Decisive test: The per-name print falsifier read at the basket's counted session-60 freeze, registered Sun 2026-11-15: each carry name is read once over its recorded legs on counted sessions up to its 60th, SURVIVES-CLEARER if name_cum_mean_net_bp is greater than 0, else DEMOTED-TO-MEMBERSHIP (basket membership and the basket verdict untouched).
Falsifier: Registered Sun 2026-11-15 in ON-FALSIFIER-SPEC.md (Amendment 2026-11-15, per-name clearer lines): per name, cum mean net of 5bp over its recorded print legs on counted sessions, read at the session-60 freeze; fewer than 60 legs read on the recorded count with the count printed; fewer than 30 legs is UNGRADED, not demoted. The amendment prints its population limit on its face: only RKLB of the named clearers sits inside the print population; FCEL, TSM and the DSR 0.9502 to 0.9874 set get no per-name print line, and their standing rides the basket accruals.
Kill rule: A name whose per-name print mean net of 5bp is not positive at its registered read leaves the clearer set back to plain basket membership.
Remaining proof: The session-60 read itself, a forward accrual from Mon 2026-11-16 on the same terms as CONV-05; the registration that was this row's blocker is filed. FCEL's long-horizon standalone form still needs about 516 sessions for a standard error of half its gross (register[106]) and stays a basket member regardless of this row. RKLB counterweight, printed in the registration: RKLB is the family's PBO carrier (register[158], confirmed by KG-3's fold anatomy), so a SURVIVES-CLEARER read is necessary evidence for any standalone registration, never the sufficient act.
First-trade condition: A clearer name trades standalone only after it prints SURVIVES-CLEARER at the session-60 freeze AND a standalone registration with its own sizing is filed; the clearer line alone promotes nothing to a trade.

#### CONV-07: FX-01 tax-loss convergence season
Source: register[1]. Tier: EXAM-PASSED. Surface: Midna's book (shares sleeves inside the season plan plus a defined-risk options satellite; the season plan is the book lane's registered construction). Status: LIVE.
Route: Trade the filed plan (midna-book/strategy-candidates/2026-11-14-forced-05-season-plan-2026.md) as written, with the three cures this program lands tonight written into this spec: the arming row (COMMITMENTS FX01-SEASON-ARMING-20261115, landed and grep-verified), the per-tranche Amendment 16 stress-loss table below, and the grading-basis sentence below. The season trades its A leg alone; the M1 substitute leg and its price proxy are excluded (M1 falsified on its own runfile: pooled arm mean -0.797 points, positive in 4 of 8 seasons).
Expression: Shares: Sleeve A (bottom-decile YTD losers from the conveyor SIP pull, equal weight) and Sleeve B (Focus 30 names at YTD at or below -15 percent at the freeze, unit weights EOSE 0.5, OKLO 0.75, others 1.0), entered at the Dec 17, Dec 23 and Dec 30 closes in 25, 25, 50 percent tranches, with the plan's capitulation accelerator. Options satellite: defined-risk bull call spreads only, long premium refused in every name; constructions of record are EOSE Jan 15 2027 2.0/2.5 (debit 0.115), FLNC Jan 15 2027 5.0/6.0 (debit 0.225), OKLO Jan 15 2027 25/30 (debit 1.375). Exits in thirds at the Jan 6, Jan 11 and Jan 13, 2027 closes, remainder Jan 15; spreads still open at the Jan 13 close are closed with the third tranche; name and basket invalidations override the schedule at any point.
Payer: Tax-motivated sellers who must realize losses before year-end regardless of price, and the January buyers who re-establish the same names; the season warehouses the names across the turn.
Execution net: Shares enter and exit at closes in staged thirds, so the cost stack is the close-print spread on beaten-down names, controlled by the amended freeze test (a name leaves if its freeze-week median quoted spread exceeds 25 percent of its ATR14). The satellite refuses long premium outright at the measured IVs (81.9 to 126.7 percent) and sells the rich upside strike inside defined-risk spreads.
Sizing: Season risk cap 0.75 percent of NAV total (registered). Per-tranche stress-loss table, written by this program to close audit gap A3 (dollar figures at the Fri 2026-11-13 NAV of $97,301.52, recomputed on freeze-date NAV): shares budget 0.50 percent of NAV (about $487) sized from the basket invalidation (a close 12 percent under the staged entry VWAP before Dec 31), giving a notional cap of about $4,058 gross split equally between Sleeve A and Sleeve B; tranche stress loss is 25, 25, 50 percent of the shares budget (about $122, $122, $243). Options satellite: maximum loss capped at the 0.25 percent probe fraction (about $243), divided equally across the three spreads (about $81 maximum loss each). Single-name cap 25 percent, gross cap 100 percent, heat cap 3 percent, daily loss stop 2 percent, all as registered in plan section 7.
Decisive test: Season 1 itself, graded Sat 2027-01-16 against the matched control on the A leg alone; there is no cheaper test for a seasonal object and none is pretended.
Falsifier: The parent registration falsifier, not softened: the December to January basket does not outperform its matched mid-pack control over the same windows in two consecutive tax seasons, or its excess concentrates in names that had January catalysts. Season 1 read Sat 2027-01-16, reported at the B5 of Fri 2027-01-22 without a verdict; verdict date is the January 2028 B5 after season 2. Season 1 kill switch: if the full-window loser-minus-control spread is worse than -3 points in season 1, the plan does not roll to season 2 on autopilot. Grading basis, written by this program to close audit gap A4: season 1 grading is on the A leg alone, the M1 substitute leg and its price proxy excluded, with the H01 prospective arm (family stock_to_peer_basket, population fixed at the Dec 16 freeze) graded beside it the same day.
Kill rule: The two-season falsifier above, plus the season 1 kill switch at a loser-minus-control spread worse than -3 points.
Remaining proof: The Dec 16, 2026 freeze itself, with three named freeze-night duties that are not tonight's work because they price December chains: (A1) spread constructions struck for any satellite name beyond EOSE, FLNC, OKLO, or the one line stating the satellite is limited to those three names by rule; (A2) the single outright-call refusal rule stated on the card as the blanket rule the plan actually applies, so the register's 82 to 124 percent figure cannot diverge from the card; (A5, cured as a pointer) the freeze procedure's H01 and M1 steps, now carried in the COMMITMENTS arming row and in this spec. EOSE enters only if it passes the freeze mortality screen (going-concern language in its Q3 2026 10-Q zeroes its shares weight; satellite at half size only).
First-trade condition: Tranche 1 at the Thu Dec 17, 2026 close, 25 percent of each sleeve weight, on the frozen lists written the night before.

#### CONV-08: aster_trader_donchian55
Source: register[3]. Tier: LIVE CANDIDATE. Surface: desk sleeve (shares, paper), live on the roster (verified in desk3 sources). Status: LIVE.
Route: Already a live desk sleeve; conversion is its promotion grade, not a build. The recorded 55/20 Donchian form trades as rostered; CONV-47 registers the Turtle 20/10 variation beside it, never instead of it.
Expression: The sleeve's recorded Donchian 55/20 breakout form on its recorded universe, desk execution.
Payer: Trend followers' counterparties: late sellers in breakouts and the impatient holders who exit before the trend leg completes.
Execution net: Profile figures are excess versus QQQ on the desk's own fill basis; no separate cost stack was printed for this sleeve, so the forward grade on live fills is the net of record.
Sizing: Existing desk sleeve sizing, unchanged. No sizing change is proposed by this program for any live sleeve.
Decisive test: The next 10 forward fires.
Falsifier: Its next 10 forward fires graded on this profile; the lead dies if forward fwd5 excess is not positive.
Kill rule: Forward fwd5 excess not positive over those 10 fires retires the candidate grade (the sleeve itself is governed by desk law, not by this register).

#### CONV-14: C06 Entry-location gate (D1)
Source: register[6]. Tier: LIVE CANDIDATE. Surface: Midna's book (component: entry conditioner on book expressions). Status: LIVE.
Route: Wired as a shadow log inside the Ghost Book and gestalt grading from Mon 2026-11-16. Conversion form: a size modifier on book entries keyed to the entry's distance off the day low in ADR units. Under book rails a veto is a size cut, so the gate's tradeable form is: entries under 0.25 ADR above the day low are cut to probe size; entries over 1.00 ADR carry full registered size; the middle band is unchanged. It never blocks a trade outright.
Expression: Conditioner, not a standalone strategy: it modifies the size of expressions the book already takes.
Payer: Chasers of extended breakouts pay the gate's user by overpaying for location; based entries keep the location edge.
Execution net: No cost stack of its own; it changes which of the book's existing expressions carry size.
Sizing: Size modifier only, inside Amendment 16 stress-loss sizing of the host expression.
Decisive test: The 45 resolved shadow reads.
Falsifier: At 45 resolved reads the gate dies if the under-0.25 bucket is not worse than the 0.50+ bucket by at least 0.3R, or if refused (cut) signals would have made money net; grade at B5 Fri 2026-12-11.
Kill rule: The falsifier above, read once at 45 resolved reads.
Remaining proof: The wired shadow accrual from Mon 2026-11-16. Historical basis is an external sample treated as an upper bound: entries under 0.25 ADR went 8.9 percent win, -0.47R; over 1.00 ADR, 40.7 percent win, +2.63R.
First-trade condition: The modifier applies to book entries only after the 45-read grade passes; until then it is logged, not applied.
Going-live disposition, Sun 2026-11-15 night (GOING-LIVE-20261115): class LIVE-CONDITIONER. Live-applied in LIVE-CONDITIONERS-20261115.md; size cut on book entries from Monday; grade and falsifier unchanged.

#### CONV-41: C2 sell the band against a held recovery stock
Source: register[31]. Tier: LEAD. Surface: Midna's book (options construction). Status: BLOCKED-ON-EXPRESSION.
Route: Pre-registered construction; its pricing data lands with the first production chain capture Mon 2026-11-16 16:46 ET (the chain panel cron), and its first read is Tue 2026-11-17, coverage and executability only, after the development lock addendum. No efficacy number exists and none is claimed; the conversion is fully specified except for the chain prices December's capture was built to provide, which is an expression block, not an evidence block. (Ordering corrected Sun 2026-11-15 night: the lock addendum's unset parameters gate the pricing, not the other way round; see Remaining proof.)
Expression: Sell the band against a held recovery stock: liability L = min(max(ST - K1, 0), K2 - K1) as registered, premium c collected against it, terminal P and L c minus L before costs.
Payer: Recovery-stock holders who overpay for upside participation above the band; the construction sells them the band.
Execution net: Unpriced until the first production chain capture; that is the named block, and no net is asserted before it.
Sizing: Defined-risk by construction (the liability is capped at K2 - K1); Amendment 16 stress loss is the capped liability per unit, sized at the probe fraction until a read exists.
Decisive test: The Tue 2026-11-17 coverage and executability read, gated on the further lock addendum named in Remaining proof; without P01 and P02 set, the read has no registered spread to grade.
Falsifier: First read Tue 2026-11-17 (coverage and executability); the construction's own registered falsifier governs any efficacy grade after that.
Kill rule: If the band cannot be sold at a premium that covers its capped liability's registered hurdle at the read, the construction stays untraded and the reason is printed.
Remaining proof: Corrected Sun 2026-11-15 night (pass2/CONV-EXPRESSION-ROUTES-2026-11-15.md, sections B1 to B3): the first unblocker is not the chain capture but a further dated lock addendum in the six-constructions program setting the strike family (P01), expiry family (P02) and conditioning fields (P05) from development data only; the filed addendum (C2C6-LOCK-ADDENDUM-2026-11-15.md) dispositions P01, P02, P03, P05, P06 and P07 as NAMED UNSET, with the explicit consequence that no credit-versus-liability read is lawful for any leg until P01 and P02 are set, so no registered spread exists to price tonight by any route. Panel coverage is NOT a gap: the held Focus chain panel carries quote-carrying call rows for 31 of 31 underlyings (12,740 call rows), with listed expiries spanning any plausible horizon band. A named decision event from the P10 flag archives is also owed. After those, the Mon 2026-11-16 16:46 ET production capture and the Tue 2026-11-17 coverage and executability read proceed as registered. One trial is charged for the C2 and C6 cell jointly, as registered.
First-trade condition: After the Tue read confirms coverage and executability and the development lock addendum is filed, at probe size.

#### CONV-43: C10 Ghost Book expression, redesigned basis (with the LS11 exit object absorbed)
Source: register[97] + register[55]. Tier: WATCH. Surface: Midna's book (options expression basis). Status: BLOCKED-ON-EXPRESSION.
Route: Redesign the resolution basis, not the signals: intraday-path resolution with stop geometry per the F20 researched levels, replacing the daily-close first-touch basis that resolved the original construction against itself (register[170]). The hold-to-expiry variant is already built (midna-book/tools/shadow_resolver_hold.py, KG-2) with settlement reruns dated after the 2026-11-27 session (20 fires) and after the 2026-12-18 session (TTMI class). The LS11 exit object is absorbed here as the redesign's exit-geometry input: on the original basis 20 of 21 exits were first-touch stops at hold 1 day (mean invalidation distance 0.578 percent of entry) while the single TARGET_HIT carried the smallest entry reward-to-risk of the 21 (0.184), so the redesign grades target proximity, not the retired viability split (which does not separate: viable 11 mean -1.000R against sub-viable 10 mean -0.789R). Update Sun 2026-11-15 night: the intraday-path arm of this redesign was graded in sample on the FULL original population and resolved against. All 21 fires were re-resolved on 1Min paths (10 from the desk cache; the other 11 fetched Sun night over the same Alpaca SIP route DH3 used, into a research scratch dir, the desk cache untouched; pass2/CONV-EXPRESSION-ROUTES-2026-11-15.md and pass2/conv43_uncovered_results.json): 0 TARGET_HIT in 21 and 21 STOPPED_OUT at -1.000R under both the all-bars and the RTH-only conventions. The retired basis's single TARGET_HIT, CLS (+1.1065R), was a daily-close artifact: on 2026-11-12 CLS printed 235.00 on its first premarket bar, through its 244.74 stop from the first bar of the day (first RTH bar open 234.44, low 233.11), and first touched its 251.17 target at 14:37Z, five and a half hours after the stop touch, closing at 256.07. The daily-close basis was hiding an intraday stop, not intraday targets. The redesign's surviving arms are the hold-to-expiry variant (settlement reruns dated) and the F20 premium arm (a flat minus 50 percent premium stop), which is not computable on this population by any held route: no intraday option marks exist for any of the 21 spreads, and on the estate's intrinsic basis the stop is breached at entry by construction (every long strike sits above its entry, intrinsic 0.000); its sources are the production chain panel from Mon 2026-11-16 forward, or a purchased intraday tape (Databento OPRA or ORATS), an owner cost decision. Second-look update Sun 2026-11-15 night (pass2/MIRAGE-SECOND-LOOKS-2026-11-15.md): two different looks attacked the first look's flaw, the daily-close basis, directly, on one uniform intraday walk (all 21 fires from fired_at through ET 2026-11-13; the fire-day tape for the 11 non-cache names was fetched over the same SIP route into research scratch only). First-hour survival conditioning: the split does not separate. Survivors of the first hour (6: HIVE, SNDK, KEEL, MU, VRT, USAR) print mean realized R -1.000 and mean pre-resolution target-distance fraction 0.3508; non-survivors (15) print -1.000 and 0.3289; pooled -1.000 and 0.3351; separations 0.000R and +0.0157 against the pre-set margins of 0.25R and 0.25, so the pre-registered kill condition fired and the entry-timing form of the rescue is closed. Gap-through census on the holding window's first bar: 20 of 21 fires were already through their stops at the first print of 2026-11-12 (the first bar's high at or below the stop, so no exit at the stop level ever existed), carrying 5,468.50 of the 5,528.50 summed debit per contract; CVNA is the sole exception, resolved later that morning. The uniform walk also corrects the CLS record: on the full tape CLS touched its stop on the fire day itself, 2026-11-11 18:18Z, 48 minutes after its stamp, and never touched its target first.
Expression: The Ghost Book's vertical debit spreads (the only expression form in the 21 resolved fires) resolved on intraday path with F20 stop geometry, and graded in parallel on the hold-to-expiry basis.
Payer: The original basis was paying the stop geometry, not the signal: intraday paths touched stops that daily closes never confirmed. The redesign's payer is the same setup flow, kept instead of donated.
Execution net: Retired basis net: 20 STOPPED_OUT at -1R and 1 TARGET_HIT in 21 fires, summed -4,875.50 per contract. Intraday-path basis on the same 21 fires (Sun 2026-11-15 night): 21 STOPPED_OUT at -1.000R, 0 TARGET_HIT, summed -5,528.50 per contract; the entire difference is CLS, the retired basis's only win, which does not survive intraday ordering. That figure is the redesign's baseline, printed so any improvement is measured against the true number.
Sizing: Host expression sizing under Amendment 16; the basis change moves no size until it grades.
Decisive test: Updated Sun 2026-11-15 night: the intraday-path arm's test is complete in sample (0 of 21; see Route). The surviving decisive reads are the hold-to-expiry settlement reruns (after 2026-11-27 for 20 fires, after 2026-12-18 for TTMI) and the F20 premium arm graded on forward fires over production-panel windows. The second looks of Sun 2026-11-15 night are complete and closed the in-sample timing question (see Route); the surviving decisive reads are unchanged.
Falsifier: The redesign's intraday-path arm is falsified in sample on its own population (0 TARGET_HIT in 21 against the retired basis's 1 in 21). The premium arm's forward grade and the hold-to-expiry settlement reruns carry the row's remaining falsifier weight; the original forward-fires falsifier for the path arm no longer has a mechanism to test and is retired with the diagnostic printed, not silently dropped. The first-hour survival arm is falsified in sample under its pre-registered margins (separations 0.000R and +0.0157 of target distance against 0.25 bars).
Kill rule: The intraday-path arm is retired by its in-sample diagnostic (0 of 21). If the hold-to-expiry reruns and the premium arm's forward grade also resolve adversely, the Ghost Book expression retires with them.
Remaining proof: Exactly two items: the hold-to-expiry settlement reruns (dated, after 2026-11-27 and after 2026-12-18) and the F20 premium arm's forward evaluation on production chain panel windows from Mon 2026-11-16 (no held intraday option marks exist for the original 21 spreads, and the panel cannot backfill 2026-11-12). The intraday-path question is closed. The second looks added no new remainder: the first-hour and gap-through questions are closed in sample, and what remains is exactly the two dated items above plus the premium arm's forward evaluation.

Source: Lane B (NEARMISS-CAMPAIGN2.md). Tier: LEAD (lane find, post-register). Surface: shadow instrument (desk refusal grader); converts to sleeves only through its own grade. Status: SHADOW-ARMED.
Route: Machinery built and proven: midna-book/tools/nearmiss_shadow.py with its spec (NEARMISS-SHADOW-SPEC.md), 14 of 14 tests passing, smoke-proven on today's 150 stamped records, grading the 344 of 1,057 historical entry near-misses that reconstruct exactly (150 stamped, 27 by intent-id suffix, 167 by unit-weight source proof; the refusals are printed too: dmac 579, LEADER1 105, multi-symbol and short 29). First manual execution Sun 2026-11-15 night: ingest read 67,814 records and added 171 stamped rows (63 equity, 108 occ) to the shadow ledger; grade printed 171 OPEN, all WINDOW_INCOMPLETE, the correct state before forward sessions accrue.
Expression: A shadow book that takes every refused entry (insufficient_slice_bp class) at its stamped terms and grades it against the taken portfolio (hypothesis H-A).
Payer: The desk's own sizing refusals are the claim: if refused entries systematically outperform taken ones, the slice rule is the tax.
Execution net: Shadow fills at stamped terms; the taken portfolio's realized record is the comparison net.

Decisive test: The 20-forward-session H-A read.
Falsifier: H-A graded at the shadow book's first read at 20 forward sessions, at a power the historical record structurally cannot give (the historical H-A test caps at 56 gradeable events and does not separate: difference -242.39bp, t -0.679).
Kill rule: H-A not separating at the read retires the conversion claim; the grader keeps running as instrumentation regardless.
Remaining proof: The cadence wiring (a scheduled run of the built machinery), then 20 forward sessions. Historical reconstruction is complete; forward accrual has not started because the cadence is unbuilt. The cadence wiring named here landed Sun 2026-11-15 night (see Route); what remains is the 20-forward-session H-A read itself.
First-trade condition: No trade attaches to the instrument. If H-A separates forward, the refusal classes that carry the separation become candidate sleeves with their own registrations; if it does not, the instrument stands as the desk's permanent refusal grader and that is a complete outcome.
Going-live disposition, Sun 2026-11-15 night (GOING-LIVE-20261115): class INSTRUMENT. Near-miss shadow book: named in the program exception class (a grading book for refused entries).

The AVOIDANCE anti-lead at register[166] (ret60 gates are load-bearing, do not drop them) is honored by construction: the parents keep their gates, and the variation carries its own falsifier.
Payer: The conjunction's own strictness is the claim: if the ret60 floor was refusing the only rows the other four conjuncts ever jointly admit, the variation collects what the parent cannot.
Execution net: Forward fills on the variation's own record are the net; no historical net exists because the parent form never fired.
Sizing: Existing desk sizing as rostered variations.
Decisive test: The weekly qualifier count, then the 20-fire variation grade.
Falsifier: The uniform variation falsifier (retire if mean fwd5 excess versus QQQ is below 0 over the first 20 resolved forward fires), preceded by the existence question: the forward check is one line per weekly bundle counting marker rows passing all five remaining conjuncts; the first week any row qualifies, the recorded policies have their first live qualifier.
Kill rule: The uniform variation falsifier at 20 resolved forward fires; zero qualifiers over a full quarter of bundles returns the anatomy question to the desk as an open row, not a silent sleeve.
Remaining proof: Forward weekly bundles from Mon 2026-11-16. Until a qualifier exists there are no fires to grade, and that state is reported as accrual, not as failure.
First-trade condition: Already live on paper as rostered variations; their first trade is their first qualifier on a forward bundle.
Going-live disposition, Sun 2026-11-15 night (GOING-LIVE-20261115): class LIVE-SLEEVE. Variations rostered live at 173d818; grade continues on forward bundles.

## 8. Retention rule and second-look states (added Sun 2026-11-15 night, owner order 20:19 ET)

**STATUS addendum, Sun 2026-11-15 night:** The second-look lane (pass2/MIRAGE-SECOND-LOOKS-2026-11-15.md) worked the three named mirages and wrote the owner's retention rule into this register. This lane changed no row's status. The register's live census at this write is SHADOW-ARMED 47, BLOCKED-ON-WIRING 2, BLOCKED-ON-EVIDENCE 2, BLOCKED-ON-EXPRESSION 4, NOT-CONVERTIBLE 4, SPEC-READY 1, total 60; it differs from section 7's census because the sibling arming lane's flips (15 BLOCKED-ON-WIRING rows to SHADOW-ARMED; CONV-27 and CONV-46 remain BLOCKED-ON-WIRING) landed in the register during this pass and are preserved exactly as written.

**The rule.** A mirage is retained as a named object with its different-look routes listed and their states. It leaves the population only when every different look is exhausted and printed, or its own pre-registered kill rule fires on a correct measurement. A look counts as different only if it attacks the first look's flaw with a different measurement; the same measurement repeated is not a different look and does not run. States: RAN-TONIGHT (the look ran in this pass), EXHAUSTED (every different look has run and is printed; the object may leave the retained population at the next census with its reads cited), NAMED-DATED (the route is named and carries a date or a dated trigger), NAMED (the route is named; its unblocker is an act, a count, or an external datum, with no fixed date), NO-DIFFERENT-LOOK (no genuinely different look exists, with the reason printed on the object's line).

**Application, counted.** Population: the 31 RESOLVED-AGAINST objects of the Potential-Edge Register (cited below by register index) plus the 4 NOT-CONVERTIBLE rows of this register, 35 objects reviewed; register[170] is the same object as the CONV-43 and CONV-19 Ghost Book arm and is counted once. Looks run tonight: 7 (3 by the second-look lane: Ghost first-hour survival, Ghost gap-through census, FIT-PIT trial 47; 4 cited from tonight's program: the Conversion Program's 38-costing options test for register[185], Wave 7 Lane B's PEAD 60-session fair fight for register[191], and Lane C's two CSMOM widenings for register[173]). Primary states: RAN-TONIGHT 4, EXHAUSTED 3, NAMED-DATED 11, NAMED 12, NO-DIFFERENT-LOOK 5. Of the 35, 25 carry at least one named future route (all 11 NAMED-DATED, all 12 NAMED, plus register[170]'s dated reruns and register[191]'s consensus axis); CONV-44 closed tonight with both forms printed and carries no future route of its own; register[185] ran tonight and reopens only on a changed chain cost stack, a condition, not a route.

Falsifier: First true two vintage disagreement read when the archive holds two vintages of the same week, about two weeks from registration; the falsifier needs two quarters of true vintages. The input claim dies if disagreement symbol weeks do not differ from the control in forward volatility or drift over those two quarters.
Kill rule: No predictive split at two quarters of true vintages retires the input claim; the capture archive stays as a data asset either way.
Remaining proof: The vintage accrual. The backward read is degenerate and is printed as such: delta z 0.0 in 152 symbol week events, sign disagreements 0 of 152, 0 quantity revisions across 232 symbol weeks, because publication is chronological in held data.

First-trade condition: The first triggered expression into the first release whose trigger has fired; if neither trigger fires, the pilot closes as untriggered at the January 2027 scoring, which is a recorded outcome, not a failure of the row.

#### CONV-109: C04 Ledger vs Surface
Source: register[94]. Tier: WATCH. Surface: Midna's book (options strategy), arming specified, gated on a dated grade. Status: LIVE.
Route: Book-options form (route 2): the ledger-minus-surface disagreement expressed as book options on the widest-spread names, but only after the object's own dated decisive test grades. Desk-shares form was considered and set aside: the claim is about the options-implied surface being wrong, so the shares form would test a different claim (that the ledger forecast is right in absolute terms) and is recorded as the losing route; if the held-out grade passes on direction but the options form cannot be priced inside its stress budget, the shares form is the named fallback registration, not a silent substitution.
Expression: After a passing grade: on each grading cycle, for the widest ledger-minus-surface spread names (in-sample leaders ACHC +14.69, ADEA +11.81, AAOI +10.79 points, re-ranked on forward data), buy the options expression the surface prices cheapest against the ledger forecast (long calls if the ledger is above the surface, long puts if below), nearest listed expiry at or beyond the forecast horizon, one name per cycle per direction.
Payer: Options market makers and surface-following hedgers who price from the implied surface alone pay the disagreement, if the private ledger carries information the surface has not absorbed.
Execution net: Design sample 34,822 graded predictions; no execution costing exists yet because no expression has been priced. Costing is part of the first grading cycle and prints before the first entry; a name whose expression cannot be priced inside its stress budget is skipped and the skip prints.
Sizing: Charter Amendment 16 stress-loss sizing: each position sized so its registered stress loss sits inside the book's single-name pod budget; no undefined-risk form in this row.
Decisive test: Sealed held-out slice, 9,491 rows, graded Thu 2026-11-19 under the Consensus Lens tests T1 to T8: population the sealed slice only; the widest-spread names' forward outcomes vs the surface-implied ranking; one page, as registered.
Falsifier: The sealed held-out slice grade on Thu 2026-11-19 under the Lens (T1 to T8, one page, registered in the record): if the held-out slice does not separate ledger-minus-surface spreads from the surface's own ranking, the row never arms and retires as a design-sample artifact. After arming, the first 10 resolved expressions at or below 0 net of costs retire the traded form.
Kill rule: A non-separating held-out grade kills the conversion before any trade. Post-arming, 10 resolved expressions at or below 0 net retire it.
Remaining proof: The dated held-out grade itself (9,491 sealed rows, Thu 2026-11-19), then 10 resolved forward expressions.
First-trade condition: The first book options entry on the first grading cycle after a passing 2026-11-19 grade; no trade before that grade, by the row's own construction.
Going-live disposition, Sun 2026-11-15 night (GOING-LIVE-20261115): class LIVE-PROGRAM. Live dated program; its first trade follows its own registered decisive test (held-out grade Thu 2026-11-19), printed as part of the strategy's form.

#### CONV-123: Graveyard revival pulses, turn-of-month pairs and PEAD
Source: register[111]. Tier: WATCH. Surface: desk sleeves (shares, paper), basket form over the three pulse families. Status: BLOCKED-ON-WIRING.
Route: Basket form (route 3), the conversion the accrual alone did not supply: the three pulses (turn-of-month, pairs, PEAD) already accrue forward under crons w7-tom-accrual-daily, w7-pairs-accrual-daily and w7-pead-accrual-daily from Mon 2026-11-16, but accrual is not a tradeable object. This row specifies the revival basket that the accrual grades into: a pulse family enters the basket only when its own frozen revival rule fires, and the basket is the conversion.
Expression: Equal-weight basket over whichever pulse families have fired their revival rule, each family traded in its published direction and published form (turn-of-month window form, pairs form, PEAD drift form), on desk paper. No family is revived early, and no buried form is edited; a family that never fires its rule never enters.
Payer: Calendar and event flow that the buried forms originally harvested: month-turn rebalancing flow for turn-of-month, relative-value convergence flow for pairs, and post-announcement drift flow for PEAD.
Execution net: Per family, the accrual's own forward fills at published form are the net of record once rostered; no net is claimed from genesis samples.
Sizing: One standard desk sleeve slice for the basket, split equally across fired families; a single fired family carries the whole slice only at half weight until a second family fires.
Decisive test: Each pulse's frozen-rule read at 250 active days or 100 events, whichever comes first per family.
Falsifier: Per pulse, the frozen revival rule as registered: 250 active days or 100 events, published-direction mean positive at t at or above 2. A family whose accrual reaches its count without meeting the rule does not enter the basket, and that non-entry is the falsifier firing, printed at the B5 of that week.
Kill rule: A family failing its frozen rule at its count stays buried and never enters the basket; the basket itself retires if every fired family grades non-positive over its first 20 basket sessions.
Remaining proof: The accruals themselves, running from Mon 2026-11-16. Genesis basis: pairs 1,469 active days at +1.04bp per active day in its genesis sample; that figure is the hypothesis the forward accrual re-earns, not a forward claim.
First-trade condition: First basket entry on the first session after the first pulse family fires its revival rule, at the sizing above, reported as a revival rostering with the rule print attached.
Going-live disposition, Sun 2026-11-15 night (GOING-LIVE-20261115): class BLOCKED. The revival rule is frozen (250 active days, or 100 events for PEAD, published-direction mean positive at t at or above 2) but the accrual ledgers are research-lane files no desk producer serves and each family's trade form lives in foundry constructions, not desk evaluators; no family can fire before its count accrues from Mon 2026-11-16. Construction per family: a desk producer per ledger plus a desk evaluator per family (turn-of-month, pairs Gatev form, PEAD drift form), as drafted in Lane B's BUGLOG text.

#### CONV-124: Guidance cuts event-level forward shadow
Source: register[112]. Tier: WATCH. Surface: Midna's book (options strategy, event expressions in shadow). Status: LIVE.

The shares-short form was priced and set aside in the ledger (borrow unpriced for event names, and the graded rolling cuts-short hold21 Sharpe is -0.507, against the event-level read).
Expression: Defined-risk long put spread on the cut name, struck from the first post-print session's chain (long put near the money, short put one step below), entered in shadow at the print-plus-one open and resolved at +5 and +21 sessions beside the accrual's equity excess lines on the same events.
Payer: Holders of cut names who absorb guidance disappointment slowly; the put spread collects the post-print repricing the event study measures.
Execution net: Entry debit at the real chain touch is part of each shadow record; resolved value net of debit is the grade, so spread cost is inside the number, not beside it.
Sizing: Shadow only until graded; on promotion, Amendment 16 stress-loss sizing at the probe fraction per event, one event expression at a time.
Decisive test: The 30-event joint grade: equity excess lines from the accrual and options resolved values from shadow_resolver on the identical event population.
Falsifier: Grade at 30 events (the register's own count) on +5 and +21 session excess versus SPY for the equity line, and on resolved spread value for the options line: the options form dies if its mean resolved value net of entry debit is not positive at 30 events, whatever the equity line prints.
Kill rule: Options line mean resolved value not positive at 30 events retires the options form; the accrual continues as an observation instrument regardless.
Remaining proof: Forward events. Event-level basis: +5 sessions mean excess -2.08 percent, t -3.13, n 14; +63 sessions -5.64 percent, t -1.76. The counterweight is printed, not smoothed: the graded rolling cuts-short hold21 series reads Sharpe -0.507, so the claim is event-level and short-horizon only, and the +21 line in the grade exists to catch exactly that decay.
First-trade condition: First live expression only after the 30-event shadow grade passes on the options line; until then every expression is a shadow resolution.
Going-live disposition, Sun 2026-11-15 night (GOING-LIVE-20261115): class LIVE-STRATEGY. Live card form in LIVE-STRATEGIES-20261115.md.

#### CONV-125: K1 Overnight complex unifying hypothesis
Source: register[114]. Tier: WATCH. Surface: desk sleeve pair form (shares, paper), on the CONV-01 population. Status: SHADOW-ARMED.
Route: Pair form (route 4) as the complex-level conversion the member rows did not get: CONV-01 to CONV-06 converted the basket and its anatomy, and CONV-48 to CONV-54 converted its conditioners, but nothing converts the hypothesis itself (two objects: one global overnight premium PLUS a separable basket-and-auction component, seat probability 0.65). The pair is the hypothesis made tradeable and gradeable: long the Focus basket overnight against a beta-matched short SPY/QQQ overnight leg, both legs on auction prints only, accruing beside the overnight falsifier tracker population from Mon 2026-11-16.
Expression: Each night: long the CONV-01 basket (auction close to auction open) and short the beta-matched benchmark overnight leg (SPY and QQQ legs weighted by the basket's fitted overnight beta over the trailing window), same prints, same sessions. The pair's nightly return is the basket component with the global premium hedged out; the unhedged basket (CONV-01) and the benchmark legs print beside it every night so the decomposition is on the face of the accrual.
Payer: The global leg's payer is the overnight warehousing payer of CONV-01; the pair's payer is whoever pays the extra premium for high-beta auction access on the Focus names specifically, if that extra premium exists.
Execution net: Both legs priced on auction prints only, never bar opens (deciding check ii of the census, already the tracker's design); the 5bp friction standard of CONV-01 applies to the pair's net line.
Sizing: Shadow pair inside CONV-01's slice accounting: the pair carries no separate capital; its grade is read off the tracker rows. If the pair grades, any traded form sizes inside CONV-01's existing slice, never in addition to it.
Decisive test: The beta-matched pair read at counted session 60 on auction prints, printed beside the unhedged basket and benchmark legs over the identical sessions; the held-data beta-match over the same 2,229 sessions (deciding check i) is run as the pair's in-sample companion and printed with its limit named.
Falsifier: At counted session 60, read with CONV-01's verdict: the two-object hypothesis dies if the pair's mean nightly return net of 5bp is not positive (the basket component is then indistinguishable from the global premium, and K1 collapses to one object, which is a complete and reportable outcome). The Lane 7 panel re-run at 20 nights rides as the scope check named in the register: if no overnight cell reproduces there, the panel's silence is printed as an 8-night scope limit, not as evidence against the pair.
Kill rule: Pair mean net of 5bp not positive at session 60 retires the two-object claim and every basket-component conditioner that cites it; CONV-01 itself is governed by its own kill rule, untouched.
Remaining proof: Forward auction-print sessions. Held-data basis for the split: C03 overnight leg on an independent universe +15.6bp; the anti-selection decomposition (universe term +272.13bp at t 6.64, idiosyncratic term -3.34bp at t -0.08) is the template the beta match follows; the single registered state variable captures most of the shared effect at 0.63, printed as the reason a hedge can isolate the basket leg at all. Lane 7 counter-read stands on the face: nothing at the overnight horizon exceeds absolute t 1.11 on the 8-night panel.
First-trade condition: No trade attaches to the pair itself. Its grade arbitrates CONV-01's anatomy claims: a surviving pair at session 60 is the evidence any basket-specific sizing argument must cite; a dead pair retires every basket-component claim while CONV-01's own demotion rule governs the basket.
Going-live disposition, Sun 2026-11-15 night (GOING-LIVE-20261115): class INSTRUMENT. K1 pair decomposition: its own construction carries no separate capital and attaches no trade to the pair; it arbitrates CONV-01's anatomy claims off tracker rows.

#### CONV-126: K2 Oversold complex unifying hypothesis
Source: register[115]. Tier: WATCH. Surface: desk sleeves (shares, paper), family basket form over the four member definitions. Status: BLOCKED-ON-WIRING.
Route: Basket form (route 3) with family arbitration as the complex-level conversion: members converted piecemeal elsewhere (C11/E3 as the CONV-17 overlay; CAND-WASH-01 as the C09 accrual vehicle) but the complex itself, one mean-reversion-after-selloff phenomenon in four parameterisations (seat probability 0.69), never got a family object. This row registers the family as ONE basket with ONE Trial Register family entry and a shared falsifier, exactly the census's deciding check ii, extended to CAND-WASH-01 as the census directs.
Expression: Family basket on desk paper: on any session where at least one member definition fires (C09 panel form, CAND-WASH-01 RSI(14) at or below 32 after a 19 to 38 percent decline, C11/E3 triple-RSI dip state, D3 QQQ RSI(3) triple filter), the basket takes the union of fired names at equal weight, deduplicated; a name fired by multiple definitions is held once, and the firing-definition set is recorded on the row so the overlap matrix accrues from live rows as well as from the held-data run.
Payer: Capitulation and forced sellers in sold-off names whose selling exhausts into the oversold state all four definitions are parameterising.
Execution net: Excess versus QQQ on the basket's own forward fills is the net of record; the published D3 figure is pre-cost and is never used as a net.
Sizing: One standard desk sleeve slice for the family basket, equal weight over the deduplicated union; member sleeves and overlays keep their own sizing and are untouched.
Decisive test: The signal-overlap matrix over one shared stored history (deciding check i): one object predicts overlap far above the independence rate; at or below independence kills the complex on held data.
Falsifier: Shared family falsifier, read once: the family dies as one object if, over its first 20 firing sessions, the basket's mean fwd5 excess versus QQQ is not positive, OR if the held-data signal-overlap matrix (deciding check i) prints overlap at or below the independence rate, in which case the one-object claim dies on held data before the forward grade and the members revert to separate grades. Either leg firing is a complete verdict on the hypothesis, printed as such.
Kill rule: Overlap at or below independence, or family fwd5 excess not positive over the first 20 firing sessions, retires the family object; no member sleeve, overlay or accrual is killed, parked or edited by this row.
Remaining proof: Two named items: (a) the held-data overlap run, all four definitions over one shared stored history with the signal-overlap matrix printed (runnable on held data, not yet run; it is this row's decisive test, not a remainder to accrue); (b) the forward family accrual from Mon 2026-11-16, re-read at 20 nights under class P3 with the family denominator, not four separate ones (deciding check iii). Basis printed at strength: seat one-object 0.69, single-variable capture only 0.48 (the capture limit is why no single conditioner substitutes for the family form); D3 published profit factor 3.54 is pre-cost and external, treated as an upper bound; C09 panel RSI=OS fwd5 +521bp at t 3.09 on 4 nights with deflation q 0.69, indistinguishable from the best of thousands of nulls, printed on the face. Seven-lens Decisions panel, Sun 2026-11-15 night (lens only): all seven lenses chose the forward-tracker route. Designed test: the signal-overlap matrix runs as the basket's tracker definition, and the basket accrues in tracker form from Mon 2026-11-16 even before the overlap run gates its rostering, so no forward sessions are lost while the held-data run waits.
First-trade condition: The family basket rosters on paper at the overlap run's print if overlap clears the independence rate; if the overlap leg kills the one-object claim, no basket trades and the members' existing conversions stand alone.
Going-live disposition, Sun 2026-11-15 night (GOING-LIVE-20261115): class BLOCKED. The decisive overlap run was not executed on guessed definitions: the four member definitions are not all stated exactly in the record (see CONV-110). Construction: one dated record stating the four definitions exactly, then the overlap run on the shared stored Focus history decides this row.

#### CONV-127: K3 Disagreement complex
Source: register[116]. Tier: WATCH. Surface: Midna's book (component: two conditioner gates on the CONV-20 host population). Status: LIVE.
Route: Conditioner form (route 8) with the complex split as the conversion: pass 1 converted only the within-read side (CONV-20, the A05 x A06 joint-below size cut). This row adds the across-agent side as a SEPARATE gate on the same host population and grades the two gates independently, which is the two-object claim (seat probability 0.78) made into an instrument. Gate W: within-read stack agreement (A05/A06 cell, already tagged). Gate X: across-sleeve agreement state from the cured intent log (symbol, side, ref_price stamped from Mon 2026-11-16), plus the chorus dispersion tag the register names as still missing.
Expression: Two independent size modifiers on forecast-driven book entries, never pooled into one disagreement score: Gate W as registered in CONV-20; Gate X cuts an entry to probe size when the intent log shows two or more distinct sleeves taking the opposite side of the same name on the same day, and carries no modifier when across-sleeve agreement is absent (its near-constant state, printed below). Each gate grades on its own rows.
Payer: Entries made where independent processes disagree pay for unresolved disagreement; each gate keeps the book from paying full size inside its own disagreement variable, and the split keeps one variable's grade from laundering the other's.
Execution net: No cost stack of its own; the host expressions carry costs.
Sizing: Size modifiers inside the host expression's Amendment 16 sizing, each gate independent; neither gate blocks an entry outright.
Decisive test: The forward correlation print between the two gate states at 150 joint rows, beside each gate's own resolved grade.
Falsifier: At 150 forward joint rows with both gates recorded: the two-object claim dies if the correlation between Gate W state and Gate X state on forward rows is at or above 0.5 (they would then be one variable wearing two tags); Gate X itself dies if its gated rows do not underperform ungated rows on the resolved sample. Two objects predicts near-zero correlation, and that prediction is the grade.
Kill rule: Correlation at or above 0.5 retires the two-object claim (the complex reverts to one conditioner, CONV-20, and Gate X is withdrawn); Gate X underperformance absent at its grade retires Gate X alone.
Remaining proof: Forward rows under the axis-tag schema with the chorus dispersion tag added (the one wiring item named in the register's state line). Scarcity printed at full strength: 849 buy-to-open fills, 39 day-symbol groups, exactly 1 with two or more distinct sleeves, so Gate X's active state is rare by construction and its grade will be slow; the Forecast Twin disagreement subset (n 8, Midna hit 0.750) is a lead at n 8 and nothing more. Single-variable capture 0.39 is printed as the reason neither gate may claim the other's rows.
First-trade condition: Gate W already logs beside entries under CONV-20. Gate X applies as a size cut only after its tag wiring lands and its first gated cohort exists; until then it is recorded, not applied.
Going-live disposition, Sun 2026-11-15 night (GOING-LIVE-20261115): class LIVE-CONDITIONER. Gate X live: chorus dispersion tag A35 added to the step-13 schema (GESTALT-AXIS-TAGS.md); Gate W rides CONV-20.

#### CONV-128: K4 Vol-overpricing complex
Source: register[117]. Tier: WATCH. Surface: Midna's book (options component: conditioner gate across the CONV-15 vol family). Status: SHADOW-ARMED.
Route: Conditioner form (route 8) as the complex-level conversion: members carry their own rows and accruals (C07 inside CONV-15, C08/G1 inside CONV-16, F5 on its own shadow accrual), but nothing converted the complex's claim, that an earnings-event effect and an index VRP effect are two objects (seat probability 0.76) sharing only the word vol. This row registers the cross-conditioner that grades exactly that: F5 earnings straddle-selling shadow events conditioned on the C05 tercile from the start (deciding check i), read beside CONV-15's gated versus unconditional index comparison, so the earnings gradient and the index gradient print in one sitting and can disagree.
Expression: Conditioner, not a strategy: every F5 shadow event is stamped with its name's C05 tercile at entry; CONV-15 expressions continue under their own gate. No pooled vol book is created, because pooling is the one-object claim this row exists to test.
Payer: Two different payers, kept separate by this row: earnings-event insurance buyers who overpay for priced moves (the C05 tercile gradient), and index variance buyers who pay implied above realised (CONV-15's payer).
Execution net: Each side's expressions price their own spread stacks on real chains; the conditioner adds no cost and claims no net.
Sizing: No sizing of its own; F5 shadow events and CONV-15 expressions keep their registered Amendment 16 sizing.
Decisive test: The 25-event F5 tercile read printed beside the CONV-15 gated versus unconditional comparison at its checkpoint, after the G1 replay verdict.
Falsifier: F5 falsifier at 25 events reads BY TERCILE, not pooled (as registered in the census): the two-object claim is confirmed if the earnings tercile gradient persists forward while the index side's grade (CONV-15 checkpoint, and G1's replay verdict repricing the index side first, deciding check ii) moves independently; the claim dies if the tercile gradient flattens to zero while a pooled vol read would have hidden the flattening, or if the two sides' forward grades correlate so tightly that one conditioner explains both. Single-variable capture 0.31 is printed as the standing warning against any pooled substitute.
Kill rule: Tercile gradient flat at 25 events with the index side persisting confirms two objects and retires any pooled vol claim; a single conditioner explaining both sides at the joint read retires the two-object claim instead. Either verdict is a complete outcome and is printed, not smoothed.
Remaining proof: Forward F5 events with C05 tercile stamps, and the G1 replay verdict (CONV-16, read due Fri 2026-11-20) landing first to reprice the index side. No own-sample pooled effect is claimed for the complex, and none is invented here.
First-trade condition: No trade attaches to the conditioner. Its grade governs whether any future vol registration may pool earnings and index expressions under one falsifier; until it prints, they may not.
Going-live disposition, Sun 2026-11-15 night (GOING-LIVE-20261115): class INSTRUMENT. Complex-level grading conditioner: no trade attaches by its own row; its decisive read waits on the G1 replay verdict (CONV-16, due Fri 2026-11-20) and forward F5 events with C05 tercile stamps, both named on the row.

#### CONV-129: Killed-Ones Index post-burial drift
Source: register[119]. Tier: WATCH. Surface: forward tracker instrument (graveyard population; no trading surface). Status: SPEC-READY.
Route: Forward tracker form, and per the task's own framing this object converts as a forward tracker or not at all: it converts as a forward tracker. Reason printed in full: every tradeable route fails on the object's own definition. Desk-shares, options, basket, pair and fade forms would all require trading the buried legs, which is un-killing by another name and is barred; a conditioner has no host, because the population is defined by having no live host. What the claim actually asserts, that buried legs drift after burial, is measurable without trading any of them, and measurement is the conversion: a pooled post-burial drift tracker over the graveyard population, read at B5 sittings. Nothing is unkilled, re-killed, resized or edited by this row.
Expression: Tracker rows: for each computable buried leg, its post-burial forward returns on the same tape and horizons its live grade used, accrued nightly from Mon 2026-11-16; pooled only WITHIN grain classes (the register's own pooling rule), never across them. Population fixed at genesis: 23 legs, 18 computable, 5 named non-computable and carried as named rows, not dropped.
Payer: Not applicable to a tracker; its yield is the desk's burial quality made measurable, so future burials and revivals price off evidence instead of memory.
Execution net: Tracker rows carry each leg's own tape returns; no execution is simulated and no net is claimed beyond the drift read itself.
Sizing: Not applicable; the tracker holds no capital at any point.
Decisive test: The first B5 drift read, Fri 2026-11-20 if the tape extends, pooled within grain classes only, with the computable count printed beside the drift.
Falsifier: First drift read at the first B5 with post-burial sessions, Fri 2026-11-20 if the tape extends (the register's dated check): the drift claim in either direction dies if the pooled within-grain drift at the first read with at least 10 post-burial sessions per grain class is within 5bp of zero per session; a signed pooled drift beyond that bar keeps the tracker running to its next doubling, and any revival argument must cite this tracker and pass through the register[111] frozen revival rule, never this row alone.
Kill rule: Pooled within-grain drift within 5bp of zero per session at the qualifying read retires the drift claim; the tracker keeps running as burial instrumentation regardless, and no leg's buried status changes under any read.
Remaining proof: Post-burial tape, which does not exist at genesis by construction: post-burial observations exactly 0, pooled drift undefined at n 0. That undefined state is the object's honest starting point and is printed rather than argued around.
First-trade condition: None, ever, under this row. The tracker's output is evidence for other lanes' registered decisions (revival pulses under register[111], burial practice at B5), not a trade.
Going-live disposition, Sun 2026-11-15 night (GOING-LIVE-20261115): class INSTRUMENT. Killed-Ones Index: a forward tracker by its own definition (first trade: none, ever, under this row); named in the deliverable's instrument list.

#### CONV-130: leaps_momentum fwd1-only pricing
Source: register[120]. Tier: WATCH. Surface: Midna's book (options strategy, forward-priced shadow). Status: SHADOW-ARMED.
Route: Book-options form (route 2), forward-priced only, with the held-data verdict printed honestly: the options form CANNOT be priced on held data by any route, because this cache's contracts carry no stored forward series past fwd1 (the record's own statement), so no held chain can carry a LEAPS holding-period net. The conversion is therefore a forward pricing instrument: from Mon 2026-11-16, each new leaps_momentum entry on its underlying tape also stamps a live-chain LEAPS expression (long call, expiry at least 6 months out, strike near the money) priced at the entry session's real chain touch, held in shadow and resolved on forward chain captures, so the holding series this cache never stored accrues from live captures instead.
Expression: Long LEAPS call per entry, shadow only, resolved at fwd5, fwd21 and fwd63 sessions on captured chains beside the underlying's own forward lines on the same entries; the underlying stream is never edited and no LEAPS position trades on the fwd1 read.
Payer: Momentum counterparties on the underlying tape; the LEAPS form's claim is only that tenor-matched convexity collects the same drift cheaper than repeated short-dated rolls, and the forward grade decides whether that claim survives theta.
Execution net: Entry debit at the real chain touch is stamped per expression; resolved value net of debit is the grade. On held data the net is unpriceable past fwd1, stated as the row's founding fact, not a caveat.
Sizing: Shadow only until graded; on promotion, Amendment 16 stress-loss sizing at the probe fraction, defined-risk by construction (long premium is the maximum loss).
Decisive test: The 15 resolved forward shadow expressions at fwd21, capture-tested against the underlying stream's excess on identical entries.
Falsifier: At 15 resolved shadow expressions (matching the fwd1 sample's n 15): the options form dies if its mean resolved value net of entry debit at fwd21 is not positive, or if it captures less than half the underlying stream's excess over the same entries, in which case the shares tape was the honest expression and the options form adds only theta.
Kill rule: Options line not positive net at fwd21, or capture under half the underlying excess, retires the options form; the underlying stream's own grade is untouched.
Remaining proof: Forward chain captures; no held-data shortcut exists and none is pretended. Basis at full strength and full limit: 16 entries, INDICATIVE tier, fwd1 +66.5 (excess +160.1, n 15) is the ONLY priced horizon; a one-session read on a LEAPS-tenor claim is a category error if traded on, which is why this row converts the pricing, not a position.
First-trade condition: First live LEAPS expression only after the 15-expression shadow grade passes at fwd21; until then every expression is a shadow pricing row.
Going-live disposition, Sun 2026-11-15 night (GOING-LIVE-20261115): class INSTRUMENT. Forward pricing instrument wired tonight (leaps-momentum-pricing-ledger.jsonl, stamps via the night study's step 7 read and the TastyTrade loader); first live expression remains gated on its own registered 15-expression grade at fwd21.

#### CONV-132: LS1 down leg character change: broad-based in Era 2, tail-carried in Era 3
Source: register[122]. Tier: WATCH. Surface: component of CONV-01 (weekend-leg conditioner grade amendment, LS1 arm). Status: SHADOW-ARMED.
Route: Conditioner form (route 8) as a grade amendment on the already-armed LS1 arm (CONV-49): the object is not a new trade but a correction to how the LS1 grade is read. The conversion specifies the amended tracker row and grade in full: every weekend-leg row carries BTC weekend move MAGNITUDE as well as sign, and the down-leg line prints median and win rate beside the mean, so a tail-carried down leg cannot be misread as a broad-based one. Expression: Amended LS1 grade: weekend legs split by BTC weekend sign AND magnitude band, with each down-leg cell printing mean, median and win rate together; no cell is graded on its mean alone.
Payer: Same weekend payer as CONV-49; the amendment's yield is that the desk stops paying for a character the forward legs do not have.
Execution net: Tracker accounting governs, as in CONV-49.
Sizing: No sizing change; CONV-49's conditioner sizing inside CONV-01's weekend legs is untouched. The amendment governs the grade that sizing waits on.
Decisive test: The 20 accrued weekend legs read on mean, median and win rate jointly, split by BTC weekend sign and magnitude.
Falsifier: Graded at the same 20-leg point as CONV-49: the character claim dies if the forward down-leg cells print mean and median of the same sign with win rate on the same side of 0.5 (a broad-based forward down leg), and the LS1 conditioner itself remains governed by CONV-49's sign-reproduction rule, read on the median line as well as the mean line from this amendment forward.
Kill rule: Forward down legs printing broad-based character (mean, median and win aligned) at the 20-leg grade retires the character-change claim; CONV-49's own kill rule stands separately.
Remaining proof: Accrued weekend legs from Mon 2026-11-16. Era anatomy printed in full: Era 3 down leg mean -50.13bp, median +8.51bp, win 0.545 (tail-carried: the mean is a few large losses, the median leg wins); Era 2 down leg mean -28.12bp, median -28.24bp, win 0.356 (broad-based). Any forward grade that printed only the mean would call these the same object at different sizes; they are different objects, which is the whole claim.
First-trade condition: No trade attaches to the amendment. CONV-49's conditioner modifies weekend-leg sizing only after the 20-leg grade, now read on the amended lines.
Going-live disposition, Sun 2026-11-15 night (GOING-LIVE-20261115): class COMPONENT. LS1 grade amendment landed in on_falsifier_tracker.py (btc_weekend_abs_bp and band on every weekend-leg row; LS1 lines print mean, median and win rate together); component of CONV-49's arm on the live basket.

Kill rule: No positive conditioned spread at session 60 retires the MK-01 conditioner use; the proxy series keeps accruing beside CLOSE-IMB-01 with no claim attached, as the record already states.

#### CONV-155: Below-flip bear-mixed quiet-window under-hit avoidance
Source: register[150]. Tier: AVOIDANCE. Surface: Midna's book (component: entry conditioner on forecast-driven entries), shadow only. Status: LIVE.
Route: Conditioner overlay on forecast-driven book entries, riding the step-13 axis tag accrual that already carries the forecast stack tags from Mon 2026-11-16 (the CONV-20 wiring). When a forecast call's stack reads below-flip bear-mixed in a quiet window, the book entry that call would drive is cut to probe size in shadow grading while the cell accrues; the conditioner never blocks an entry outright, per book rails that a veto is a size cut.
Expression: Conditioner keyed to the exact cell: below-flip, bear-mixed, quiet window, definition held verbatim from ORDERUP45-DIG.md.
Payer: Forecast-driven entries made in this cell pay for their own disagreement; the conditioner keeps the book from paying full size for a cell that under-hits by 25.67pp.
Execution net: Hit rate object, no bp gross to price a fade against: a directional fade of the calls would need a per call bp series the record does not carry, so no fade is claimed. The conditioner has no cost stack of its own; its cost is the entries it cuts, graded on the same forward rows.
Sizing: Size modifier inside the host expression's Amendment 16 stress-loss sizing; shadow logged until the grade below passes.
Decisive test: The 40 forward rows in the exact cell, read once.
Falsifier: Re-grade at 40 forward rows in the exact cell (the register's own check). The conditioner dies if the forward cell hit rate is not below the forward all-rows hit rate, or if the cut entries would have made money net on the resolved sample.
Kill rule: The falsifier conditions above, read at 40 forward rows, retire the conditioner.
Remaining proof: The forward accrual. Retrospective basis: 8 of 20 hits, rate 0.40, excess -25.67pp, z -2.418, q 0.6026 over 10 nights; wider set 12 of 26, excess -19.51pp, z -2.096. The q of 0.6026 is the limiting number: this is a shadow conditioner, not a promoted rule, until the forward rows grade.
First-trade condition: The size cut applies to a live book entry only after the 40 row forward grade passes; until then it is logged beside entries.
Going-live disposition, Sun 2026-11-15 night (GOING-LIVE-20261115): class LIVE-CONDITIONER. Live-applied avoidance size cut, definition verbatim from ORDERUP45-DIG.md.

#### CONV-158: confluence exits avoidance at 5 sessions
Source: register[153]. Tier: AVOIDANCE. Surface: exit overlay spec on the confluence sleeve, graded in the book's exit-why instrument (exit_why_grader.py). Status: SHADOW-ARMED.
Route: Registered exit overlay: the confluence exit is recorded as a validated exit class on its host sleeve, and every forward exit on that sleeve is graded on its post-exit path in exit_why_grader.py, the existing instrument for exit class grading. The claim converts in the overlay direction, not the fade direction: the measured fact is that after these exits the names keep falling at 5 sessions (post5 -275.0bp, clustered t -2.95), so the exit is the earning side. A short-after-exit fade is priced in the ledger and left unclaimed because the record names no underlying to price borrow or spread against.

Payer: Whoever buys the host sleeve's exited names into the 5 session slide the exit avoided; the overlay's yield is the avoided -275.0bp post5 path, forward graded.
Execution net: Fade priced, not claimed: shorting at the exit print grosses +275.0bp at post5; break even borrow is about 138.6 percent per year over 5 sessions, so borrow alone cannot erase it, but the underlying is unnamed in the record, so neither its borrow fee nor its exit print spread is priceable, and no net is asserted. The overlay form needs no such price: its net is the host's own realized exit record, graded forward.
Sizing: Overlay only; it changes no exit, size or threshold on the host tonight.
Decisive test: The next 20 priceable exits, mean post5 excess read.
Falsifier: The register's own falsifier is the overlay's: over the next 20 priceable exits, if mean post5 excess is at or above 0, the exit class validation retires.
Kill rule: Mean post5 excess at or above 0 over those exits retires the exit class validation.
Remaining proof: The next 20 priceable exits on the one host sleeve. Historical: 52 exit fills, 27 priceable at post5 over 3 exit dates; post1 +287.3bp (n 32), post5 -275.0bp (clustered t -2.95), post10 unpriceable (n 0), cut share 0.333, realized mean -95.2bp (n 30). The 3 exit date clustering is the limiting number.
First-trade condition: No new trade. The overlay's first live consequence, only after a passing grade, would be a desk lane proposal to extend the confluence exit class to sleeves sharing its exit geometry, reported as a new variation question, never applied from this lane.
Going-live disposition, Sun 2026-11-15 night (GOING-LIVE-20261115): class INSTRUMENT. Confluence exit overlay graded in exit_why_grader (program exception instrument family).

Source: register[158]. Tier: AVOIDANCE. Surface: component of CONV-01 and CONV-05 (desk overnight baskets, shares, paper), shadow basket arm. Status: LIVE.
Route: Basket exclusion arm beside CONV-01: the falsifier tracker's population runs a second shadow basket, the Focus basket with RKLB excluded and the remaining names re-weighted equally, graded on the same counted sessions and the same 5bp friction standard as CONV-01 and CONV-05. This is the basket level conversion of a basket level fact (the family PBO is carried by RKLB winner splits). It complements and does not duplicate CONV-06, whose per-name clearer line grades RKLB itself at the same freeze: this arm grades what the family looks like without it.
Expression: Shadow basket: CONV-01's overnight auction form over the Focus names minus RKLB, equal weight over the survivors, closing auction in and opening auction out.
Payer: Same overnight warehousing payer as CONV-01; the arm claims part of the family's apparent robustness is one name's in-sample winning, and prices that claim forward.
Execution net: A directional fade is priced and rejected: shorting RKLB overnight shorts a leg whose own gross is +21.5bp per night (CONV-06), before the roughly 5.8bp carry basket auction cost and any borrow, so the short is negative on the object's own numbers. The basket arm's net is tracker accounting at the registered 5bp friction, identical basis to CONV-01.
Sizing: Shadow only; no capital and no change to CONV-01's live basket composition or weights tonight.
Decisive test: The session-60 freeze read, exclusion arm against full basket on identical counted sessions.
Falsifier: Read once at CONV-01's counted session-60 freeze: the exclusion arm earns its place only if its mean net of 5bp is at or above the full basket's mean net of 5bp on the same counted sessions; if it is below, the exclusion retires and RKLB's standing is governed by CONV-06's per-name line alone.
Kill rule: Exclusion arm mean net of 5bp below the full basket's at the freeze retires the exclusion.
Remaining proof: The 60 session accrual from Mon 2026-11-16 on the tracker population. Historical anatomy: removing ON_1n_RKLB drops family PBO from 0.2222 to 0.0444 (bad splits 10 to 2); removing RKLB plus TSM prints PBO 0.0000; RKLB is the in-sample winner in 10 splits, 9 of them bad. Counterweight printed on the face, from CONV-06: RKLB's own overnight leg is a clearer (DSR 0.9997, Sharpe 1.59, t 4.74, +0.215 percent per night gross), so this arm tests family fragility, it does not assert RKLB is a bad leg.
First-trade condition: No standalone trade. If the exclusion arm wins at the freeze and CONV-06's line demotes RKLB, a basket registration without RKLB becomes a desk lane proposal; if the two reads diverge, the divergence is reported and neither is reconciled away.
Going-live disposition, Sun 2026-11-15 night (GOING-LIVE-20261115): class LIVE-SLEEVE. Rostered live as overnight_focus29_ex_rklb (Focus basket minus RKLB, equal weight over survivors) on the same basket engine; live resolution 28 of 28.

#### CONV-168: Sweep wide-range extreme-ATR cluster overnight hazard
Source: register[164]. Tier: AVOIDANCE. Surface: component (overnight hold hazard overlay on desk sleeve holdings and book overnight holds), shadow only. Status: SHADOW-ARMED.
Route: Hazard overlay on overnight holds, riding the step-13 axis tag accrual that grades this cluster forward at 20 nights (the register's own mechanism). When a name held into the night prints the hazard state at the close (RNG10=WIDE and ATRPCT=EXT, with the POS10=BOT, MACD bearish and RS10 weak sub-states recorded), the overlay records a shadow close-exit for that name-night beside the actual hold, and grades whether avoiding the hazard name-nights beats holding them. The register's own boundary is honored on the face: never a short entry; this is a hold avoidance overlay, not a fade.
Expression: Overlay keyed to the sweep axis tags (RNG10, ATRPCT, POS10, MACD, RS10) on any position held overnight, desk paper sleeves and book overnight holds alike, in shadow.
Payer: The overnight tape in wide-range extreme-ATR names: whoever holds them into the night pays the hazard; the overlay's yield is the avoided negative overnight path, forward graded.
Execution net: Fade priced and rejected on the register's own instruction and numbers: shorting the hazard names grosses up to +271.6bp on the top cell, but the top cells rest on 7 nights, the single named cell fails its q (0.9995), and the register states the cluster is a hazard state, never a short entry; a hazard (a state where holding is dangerous in both directions) does not price a directional short. The overlay's net is the paired shadow exit versus hold difference on forward name-nights, which needs no borrow and crosses no spread in shadow.
Sizing: Overlay only; it sizes nothing and exits nothing live tonight. A passing grade would make it a size or hold conditioner proposal under the host's own rails (desk sizing on the desk, Amendment 16 on the book).
Decisive test: The 20 forward nights of step-13 axis tag accrual, hazard versus non-hazard held name-nights.
Falsifier: At 20 forward nights of axis tag accrual: the overlay retires if the hazard state name-nights do not underperform the non-hazard held name-nights on the forward sample, or if the shadow close-exit arm does not beat the hold arm on hazard name-nights.
Kill rule: The falsifier conditions above, read at 20 forward nights, retire the overlay.
Remaining proof: The 20 night forward accrual from Mon 2026-11-16. Historical: 84 graded sweep cells negative at night at t at or below -2, 11 of them in the RNG10=WIDE x ATRPCT=EXT family; top cells with POS10=BOT -271.6bp (t -4.80, 7 nights), with MACD bearish -262.8bp (t -4.76), with RS10 weak -256.9bp (t -4.23); single cell SW-10475 -309.2bp, night t -10.41, on 7 observations over 4 nights at q 0.9995, whose q is printed as its limit.
First-trade condition: No trade attaches to the overlay. A live hold rule may be proposed only after the 20 night grade passes, as a host lane decision.
Going-live disposition, Sun 2026-11-15 night (GOING-LIVE-20261115): class INSTRUMENT. Overnight hazard overlay: a hold-avoidance read whose live form is reserved to per-host lane decisions by its own row; never a short entry.

Kill rule: Inverted forward fwd5 excess not positive at the read retires the arm; the base sleeve is untouched.
Remaining proof: The 20 forward fires. Historical: 27 entries, INDICATIVE to SCORED on the re-base; negative raw and excess at fwd1, fwd3 and fwd5; inversion +226.0bp.
