## 9. CONV-27 AND CONV-46 UNBLOCKER LANDINGS, SUN 2026-11-15 NIGHT

Owner order Sun 2026-11-15 20:32 ET: figure out those 2 rows. Both unblockers named in section 8 were executed as proper desk landings, sequentially, under the arming lane's discipline (BUGLOG entries with rollbacks, gate-wired regression blockers with fail direction proven on git-archive copies of the pre-change trees, commits by pathspec, pushes via the Git Data API mirror route with remote shas verified by fresh reads). Census after this update, recounted from the rows: the original 60 conversion rows read SHADOW-ARMED 49, BLOCKED-ON-WIRING 0, BLOCKED-ON-EXPRESSION 4, NOT-CONVERTIBLE 4, BLOCKED-ON-EVIDENCE 2, SPEC-READY 1, total 60; the full 169-row file reads SHADOW-ARMED 98, BLOCKED-ON-WIRING 24, BLOCKED-ON-EXPRESSION 4, NOT-CONVERTIBLE 16, BLOCKED-ON-EVIDENCE 2, SPEC-READY 25.

- CONV-27 (ENJ breakout stream): SHADOW-ARMED. Root cause, proven by direct evaluation on the live-assembled view: desk3/aster_batch7.py's private bar reader tried only archive spellings no producer serves and returned 0 bars on every live snapshot, while every sibling Aster crypto engine resolves the served Kraken spot tape through desk3/crypto_bars.resolve_bars (721 bars live). Cure (ENJ-FEED-CONV27-20261115, desk commit a090950183dd964208692cd0bdb8eaa224b419f8, gate receipt 20261116T015555Z-9455, Ran 2361 tests OK): batch7 delegates to the family resolver (archive keys still win inside it; no ENJ sleeve's logic, sizing, stops or thresholds changed); the warm layer persists the Kraken spot tape as ENJ/USD|4Hour (540 bars, fail-closed); the conversion grader prices ENJ on that tape and pools dispatcher entry intents as shadow entries for the arm (ENJ/USD is unlisted at the broker, so entries fail closed at the broker seam and can never fill; the register's named step grades those intents as shadow entries). The arm's falsifier and kill rule are byte-identical to this row's, decisive horizon fwd10 per the row's own profile. Live proof: the production warm cache carries the tape from the 2026-11-16T02:34:07Z fetch, the grader prices 91 ENJ daily closes from it, the dry run shows CONV-27 ACCRUING, and all 22 ENJ sleeves read FED on the live health roll. Sole remainder: the forward accrual itself, graded at the first 10 resolved entries at fwd10 from Mon 2026-11-16.
- CONV-46 (SSO TIME_25 removal variation): SHADOW-ARMED (rostered). The unblocker's strict order was kept: fidelity first, rostering only on proof. desk3/sso_opus.py is a line-faithful port of the 2026-11-03 research baseline; the fidelity proof (pass2/sso_fidelity_check.py, pass2/sso_fidelity_result.json) shows the original harness reproducing its own ledgers exactly and the port reproducing both ledgers trade-for-trade (265/265 primary at cost 0, 282/282 at cost 5bp, 0 divergences at 1e-9) plus the synthetic known-answer series (4/4). Only then were sso_opus (the baseline) and sso_opus_notime25 (the variation: the same engine with exactly the TIME_25 exit class removed, its own id, this row's falsifier and kill rule byte-identical in config/conversion_variations_20261115.json) rostered, roster 359 to 361 (SSO-PORT-CONV46-20261115, desk commit 6e9441ba8f0f9d4064d834e84814f2926e6f9075, gate receipt 20261116T032233Z-11615, Ran 2373 tests OK; the gate ran with a sibling lane's uncommitted shadow-registry file set aside byte-exact and restored untouched, named in the BUGLOG). Live proof: the warm cache carries SSO|15Min (1,224 bars), and the health roll at 2026-11-16T03:18:55Z carries both sleeves FED with status ok (353 FED / 8 UNFED of 361). The variation's historical anatomy (242 trades, total +110.7 percent, TIME_60 175 / STOP_LOSS 42 after the removal) is context only; its grade is the registered paired forward falsifier. Sole remainder: the paired forward grade itself, the variation's first 20 forward fires against the baseline's same-day fires from Mon 2026-11-16.
Going-live disposition, Sun 2026-11-15 night (GOING-LIVE-20261115): class GRADER-ARM. Forward inversion arm on the CONV-39 funnel route. Live inverted form BLOCKED on the identical construction as CONV-39: t_cci_corr is a hash-pinned port with no invert switch and no inverted evaluator exists.
